## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我去修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，我弄清楚了再告诉你。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner for work on this machine. For example, I can fix a failing test in a repo while you do something else, and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 특정 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하면 제가 작업을 열어 처리하고, 끝나면 결과를 알려 드려요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 감사합니다. 내일 아침에 봐요.
--- step 2: user says: OK

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP は届いたことを確認しながら順番どおりに送るプロトコルで、UDP は確認や順序保証をせずに速さを優先して送るプロトコルです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner tu rama al día con la base (por ejemplo, `main`) y mantener un historial lineal, siempre que la rama sea solo tuya y aún no la hayas compartido. Usa merge cuando la rama ya sea compartida o quieras conservar en el historial el momento exacto en que se integró.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接、按顺序交付的字节流（会重传、确认、控制流量和拥塞）；UDP 是无连接、不保证送达和顺序的数据报，开销小、速度快，但丢了也不补。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want your branch's commits to sit on top of the latest base as one clean line of history, usually for your own unpublished work. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rebasing rewrites commits others may have pulled.
```

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
```

### board-offer-go-zh

```
(earlier) user: 每天早上帮我看一下周五北京到上海的高铁票价，600 以下的告诉我。
(earlier) partner ack: 好，每天早上去看一次。  → task "每天看周五京沪高铁票价" working; its report: 第 3 天：周五 G1 553 元（二等座余 12 张），G3 553 元（余 4 张），G7 626 元；600 以下的有 2 班。
(earlier) partner: 周五 600 以下的还有两班：G1 和 G3，都是 553 元，G3 只剩 4 张。
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "fares"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，周五京沪高铁的看板已经填入 G1、G3、G7 的票价和余票，记一笔就自动加总。
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁看板", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2459 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <span class="k-sum-label">周五 京沪 600 元以下</span>
  <span class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">—</span>
  <span class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</span>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">车次</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>车次</label><input class="k-input" id="no" placeholder="如 G1"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>票价（元）</label><input class="k-num" inputmode="decimal" id="price"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>余票（张）</label><input class="k-input" id="left"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">加上</button>
</details>
<script>
var LIMIT = 600;
function trains() { return ducter.get('trains') || []; }
function esc(s) { return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function(c) { return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]; }); }
function render() {
  var t = trains();
  var under = t.filter(function(x) { return Number(x.price) < LIMIT; });
  document.getElementById('cnt').textContent = under.length + ' 班';
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = under.length
    ? under.map(function(x) { return x.no; }).join('、') + ' 600 元以下'
    : '暂时没有 600 元以下的';
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!t.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">还没记，每天早上的结果会填在这里</div>';
    return;
  }
  t.forEach(function(x) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(x.no) +
      (x.left ? '<div class="k-row-sub">余 ' + esc(x.left) + ' 张</div>' : '') +
      '</div><div class="k-row-value">¥' + esc(x.price) + '</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', function() {
  var no = document.getElementById('no').value.trim();
  var price = document.getElementById('price').value.trim();
  var left = document.getElementById('left').value.trim();
  if (!no || !price) return;
  var t = trains().filter(function(x) { return x.no !== no; });
  t.push({ no: no, price: Number(price), left: left });
  ducter.set('trains', t);
  document.getElementById('no').value = '';
  document.getElementById('price').value = '';
  document.getElementById('left').value = '';
});
ducter.onChange(render);
ducter.summary('600 元以下 ' + trains().filter(function(x) { return Number(x.price) < LIMIT; }).length + ' 班');
render();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"left":"12"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"left":"4"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"left":""}]}
  [Ducter passed the user's answer to task fares] [Ducter] The person now keeps this work's rounds in their small app "周五京沪高铁看板" (app ac755311b). Each round, write what stands now into it with app_data (app ac755311b, set {key: value}; null removes a key); the app reads the keys trains. Write whole values (a key's value is replaced), once a round. Then don't report_to_main for a round that only updated it: report only when something needs the per
  [also used tools] app
```

```
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);

  var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('latest').textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('latestSub').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · ' + last.night;
  ducter.summary(last.night + ': ' + last.writes + ' writes/s');
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
```

### app-make-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 给我做个小应用，记一下这趟北京出差的花销，按天列，带个合计
  [partner message] 做好了，记一笔就自动加总，按天分开列。
  [an app card under the message: made "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 3551 characters): <style>[hidden]{display:none!important}</style>
<main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">记了一笔，就按天列在下面</div>
  </section>

  <h2 class="k-h">按天</h2>
  <div id="days"></div>

  <details class="k-add" id="panel">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="day">第几天</label>
      <input class="k-num" id="day" inputmode="numeric" placeholder="1">
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="name">花在哪</label>
      <input class="k-input" id="name" placeholder="午饭、地铁、酒店…">
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="amt">金额（元）</label>
      <input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0">
    </div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
    <button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删除这一笔</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
let items = [];
let editing = -1;
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
const fmt = n => Number(n).toLocaleString('z … 列在这里。</div>';
}

function reset() {
  editing = -1;
  $('name').value = '';
  $('amt').value = '';
  $('add').textContent = '记下';
  $('del').hidden = true;
  $('panel').open = false;
}

function save() { ducter.set('items', items); }

async function load() {
  const v = await Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items'));
  items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
}

$('days').onclick = e => {
  const r = e.target.closest('[data-i]');
  if (!r) return;
  editing = Number(r.dataset.i);
  const x = items[editing];
  $('day').value = x.day;
  $('name').value = x.name;
  $('amt').value = x.amt;
  $('add').textContent = '保存';
  $('del').hidden = false;
  $('panel').open = true;
};

$('add').onclick = () => {
  const day = parseInt($('day').value, 10);
  const name = $('name').value.trim();
  const amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if (!day || day < 1 || !name || isNaN(amt) || amt < 0) return;
  const rec = {day, name, amt};
  if (editing >= 0) items[editing] = rec; else items.push(rec);
  reset();
  save();
  render();
};

$('del').onclick = () => {
  if (editing < 0) return;
  items.splice(editing, 1);
  reset();
  save();
  render();
};

ducter.onChange(() => load());
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

```
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=xs.length?"¥"+total:"¥0";
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";

}

  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
```

```
  </div>

  <h2 class="k-h">By category</h2>

  <details class="k-add" id="addbox">
    <summary>Add a spend</summary>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="amt">Amount</label>
      <input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0.00">
    </div>
    <div class="k-chips" id="chips"></div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="note">What for</label>
      <input class="k-input" id="note" placeholder="Lunch, bus, coffee…">
    </div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">Add</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
const cats = ['Food', 'Transport', 'Shopping', 'Bills', 'Other'];
let spends = [];
let cat = cats[0];
let openId = null;
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const fmt = n => n.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>' … omise.resolve(ducter.set('spends', spends)).then(render);
}

$('#chips').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const b = e.target.closest('[data-cat]');
  if (!b) return;
  cat = b.dataset.cat;
  render();
});

$('#add').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const amount = parseFloat($('#amt').value);
  if (!(amount > 0)) return;
  spends.push({
    id: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6),
    amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100,
    cat,
    note: $('#note').value.trim(),
    at: Date.now()
  });
  $('#amt').value = '';
  $('#note').value = '';
  $('#addbox').open = false;
  save();
});

$('#list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if (del) {
    spends = spends.filter(s => s.id !== del.dataset.del);
    openId = null;
    save();
    return;
  }
  const row = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
  if (!row) return;
  openId = openId === row.dataset.id ? null : row.dataset.id;
  render();
});

function load(v) {
  spends = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('spends')).then(load);
if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(() => Promise.resolve(ducter.get('spends')).then(load));
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
```

```
(function () {
  var KEY = 'feeds';
  var feeds = ducter.get(KEY) || [];

    var d = new Date();
    return d.getFullY …     el('addbox').open = false;

    editing = f;

    el('ml').value = f.ml;
    el('addtitle').textContent = '改这一笔';
    el('del').hidden = false;
    el('cancel').hidden = false;
    el('addbox').open = true;
    el('addbox').scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });

    var ml = parseFloat(el('ml').value);

      editing.ml = ml;
    } else {
      feeds.push({ d: today(), t: t, ml: ml });
    }
    ducter.set(KEY, feeds);
    clearForm();
    render();
  }

  function remove() {
    if (!editing) return;
    feeds = feeds.filter(function (f) { return f !== editing; });
    ducter.set(KEY, feeds);
    clearForm();
    render();
  }

  el('save').addEventListener('click', save);
  el('del').addEventListener('click', remove);
  el('cancel').addEventListener('click', clearForm);

  ducter.onChange(function () {
    feeds = ducter.get(KEY) || [];
    render();
  });

  render();
})();
```

```
}

}

  ducter.set("prs",items);
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>
```

```
    setCat(x.cat || "交通");
    $("save").textContent = "保存修改";
    $("del").style.display = "";
    $("add").open = true;
    $("amt").scrollIntoView({block: "center"});
  }

  $("save").onclick = () => {
    const amt = Number($("amt").value);
    if (!$("amt").value || isNaN(amt)) return;
    const xs = items();
    const rec = {
      trip: $("trip").value.trim(),
      day: $("day").value.trim(),
      cat: cat,
      amt: amt
    };
    if (editing >= 0) {
      const old = xs[editing];
      if (old && old.note) rec.note = old.note;
      if (old && old.from) rec.from = old.from;
      xs[editing] = rec;
    } else {
      xs.push(rec);
    }
    ducter.set("items", xs);
    resetForm();
    $("add").open = false;
    draw();
  };

  $("del").onclick = () => {
    if (editing < 0) return;
    const xs = items();
    xs.splice(editing, 1);
    ducter.set("items", xs);
    resetForm();
    $("add").open = false;
    draw();
  };

  ducter.onChange(draw);
  draw();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南","from":"你的预订"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","from":"你的预订"}]}
```

```
--- step 1: user says: 帮我查一下 12 月 20 号到 23 号京都有什么安静点的住处，看好了把价格告诉我
  [partner ack line] 好，我去查 12 月 20 到 23 号京都安静一点的住处和价格，查清楚了告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 京都 12/20–12/23 安静住处查价 | place: {"kind":"browser","profile":"default","device":"local"} | runs on computer: the home (Ducter's home, called "local" in a place) | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (sonnet) | brief: 用户原话：“帮我查一下 12 月 20 号到 23 号京都有什么安静点的住处，看好了把价格告诉我”。 ⏎  ⏎ 要做的事： ⏎ - 查 2026 年 12 月 20 日入住、12 月 23 日退房（3 晚）京都的住宿。 ⏎ - 偏好安静的地方（住宅区、离主干道和夜生活区远、不临街喧闹的民宿或酒店、有安静评价的房源）。 ⏎ - 挑出几处看好的，列出：名称、区域、每晚价格和 3 晚总价（含税费，标明是否含税）、是否可取消、评价里提到安静的依据。 ⏎ - 最后给出你最推荐的一处和理由。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准：给出至少 3 个候选及其价格，每个价格注明来源页面和查询日期；说明哪些是我无法确认的（比如价格未含税、房态不明）。 ⏎  ⏎ 范围之外：不要预订、不要提交任何表单、不要付款、不要登录账号。如果需要登录才能看到价格，停下来问用户，不要自己登录。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：默认浏览器，公开网页即可查看。
--- step 2: task "$opened0" reports done: Two fits: Tawaraya ryokan, ¥180,000 for 3 nights with breakfast; Hotel The Celestine Gion, ¥96,000 for 3 nights. Both quiet, near Gion. | (no evidence attached)
  [partner message] 查到两处：一家是俵屋旅馆，3 晚 18 万日元，含早餐；另一家是 Hotel The Celestine Gion，3 晚 9.6 万日元，都在祇园附近，评价里说比较安静。这只是查询任务的结论，它没附上页面截图或记录，价格和房态要以订房页面为准。
```

### wp-pr-babysitter-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 帮我启动一个 codex 的 loop agent，名字就叫 Nowledge Mem PR babysitter（之类的名字），在 Nowledge Bridge 里你起好名字，我可以让我自己的 agent 在 PR 到一定阶段之后把它们丢给你，然后你就可以帮忙盯着，做需要的修改修复 ci failure，或者如果涉及到别的人的 review，帮忙做修复，如果你能自决修复的，就不断修复 push 然后看着，直到工作可以被合并，再 bridge 回信表示工作做完了

我们 bridge 不知道是否有唤醒能力，如果没有，暂时需要你自己去 loop 唤醒自己读取 bridge 信箱
  [partner message] 好，已经建好了，名字叫「Nowledge Mem PR babysitter」。你的 agent 在 PR 到一定阶段后，通过 Nowledge Bridge 把它交给这个名字就行。它收到后会自己盯 CI 和 review，能自己修的就修、push 后接着盯，做完回信给派活的 agent。

唤醒不用你操心，Ducter 会负责叫醒它，不需要我们自己轮询信箱。地址会在 设置 › Agent 通信 里看到。
  [also used tools] helper
--- what Ducter sent through Bridge (other agents see this, the user doesn't):
```
